/**\n   247→   *  Sender dequeues LoggingAuditEvents and sends them to Kafka cluster. If send to one partition\n   248→   *  fails, it will choose another partition. For each event, it will try at most\n   249→   *  NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING times (3 times) before dropping this event. public void checkAndEnqueueWhenSendFailed() {\n   310→      // if exception thrown (i.e. the send failed), the partition is added to badPartitions.\n   311→      badPartitions.add(this.partition);\n   312→      OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n   313→          .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_PARTITION_ERROR, 1,\n   314→              \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(), Drop this event.\");\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n        }\n      } catch (InterruptedException ex) {\n        LOG.debug(\n            \"Enqueuing LoggingAuditEvent at head of the queue was interrupted in callback. \"\n                + \"Drop this event\");\n        eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n      }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onCompletion(RecordMetadata recordMetadata, Exception e) {\n      try {\n        if (e == null) {\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n              .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_ACKED, 1,\n                  \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                  \"logName=\" + event.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName());\n\n          // if send is successful, remove the event from the map eventTriedCount if it was added\n          // LoggingAuditHeaders can uniquely identify an event.\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n          // if send out successfully, remove the partition from the badPartitions if it was added.\n          badPartitions.remove(recordMetadata.partition());\n        } else {\n          checkAndEnqueueWhenSendFailed();\n        }\n      } catch (Throwable t) {\n        LOG.warn(\"Exception throws in the callback. }\n    }\n    resetCurrentPartitionIdIfNeeded();\n  }\n\n  private void resetCurrentPartitionIdIfNeeded() {\n    if (partitionInfoList.size() == 0) {\n      currentPartitionId = -1;\n      return;\n eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (InterruptedException e) {\n        LOG.warn(\"[{}] got interrupted when polling the queue and while loop
